Injected direct transports are test-only.\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n\r\n    def __init__(self, worker=_http_worker, grace=0.2, max_bytes=2 * 1024 * 1024):\r\n        if not _positive(grace) or not _integer(max_bytes) or max_bytes < 1:\r\n            raise LLMError(\"configuration\", \"Worker grace and response size limit must be positive.\")\r\n        self.worker = worker\r\n        self.grace = grace\r\n        self.max_bytes = max_bytes\r\n        self.events = []\r\n\r\n    def __call__(self, endpoint, body, key, timeout):\r\n        end = time.monotonic() + timeout\r\n        if not _positive(timeout) or timeout <= 2 * self.grace:\r\n            raise LLMError(\"deadline\", \"Insufficient time for worker cleanup.\")\r\n        context = multiprocessing.get_context(\"spawn\")\r\n        buffer = context.RawArray(\"B\", (self.max_bytes * 4 // 3) + 8192)\r\n        process = context.Process(target=self.worker, args=(buffer, endpoint, body, key,\r\n                                                          end - 2 * self.grace, self.max_bytes))\r\n        process.daemon = True\r\n        launched = False\r\n        try:\r\n            if time.monotonic() >= end - 2 * self.grace:\r\n                raise LLMError(\"deadline\", \"Worker setup consumed the dispatch allowance.\")\r\n            process.start()\r\n            launched = True\r\n            self.events.append({\"event\": \"start\", \"pid\": process.pid, \"at\": time.monotonic()})\r\n            process.join(max(0, end - time.monotonic() - 2 * self.grace))\r\n            if process.is_alive():\r\n                raise LLMError(\"timeout\", \"Request deadline reached; remote billing is unknown.\", True)\r\n            if process.exitcode != 0:\r\n                raise LLMError(\"worker_failure\", \"Request worker exited without a result.\", True)\r\n            length = struct.unpack(\"!Q\", bytes(buffer[:8]))[0]\r\n            if not 0 < length <= len(buffer) - 8:\r\n                raise LLMError(\"worker_failure\", \"Request worker returned incomplete IPC data.\", True)\r\n            result = _strict_json(bytes(buffer[8:8 + length]))\r\n            if \"error\" in result:\r\n                code = result[\"error\"]\r\n                raise LLMError(code, \"Request worker could not return a complete response.\",\r\n                               code in (\"network\", \"worker_failure\"))\r\n            return result[\"status\"], {\"Retry-After\": result[\"retry_after\"]}, base64.b64decode(result[\"body\"])\r\n        except LLMError:\r\n            raise\r\n        except Exception:\r\n            raise LLMError(\"worker_failure\", \"Could not start or read the request worker.\") from None\r\n        finally:\r\n            if launched:\r\n                if process.is_alive():\r\n                    self.events.append({\"event\": \"terminate\", \"pid\": process.pid, \"at\": time.monotonic()})\r\n                    try:\r\n                        process.terminate()\r\n                    except OSError:\r\n                        pass  # Race with exit; still join and escalate if alive.\r\n                    process.join(self.grace)\r\n                if process.is_alive():\r\n                    self.events.append({\"event\": \"kill\", \"pid\": process.pid, \"at\": time.monotonic()})\r\n                    try:\r\n                        process.kill()\r\n                    except OSError:\r\n                        pass\r\n                    process.join(self.grace)\r\n                if process.is_alive():\r\n                    # Fatal: caller must stop, never dispatch another worker.\r\n                    raise LLMError(\"worker_cleanup\", \"Operating system did not reap the terminated worker.\")\r\n                self.events.append({\"event\": \"reaped\", \"pid\": process.pid,\r\n                                    \"at\": time.monotonic(), \"exitcode\": process.exitcode})\r\n            process.close()\r\n\r\n\r\n    def __init__(self, transport=None, clock=time.monotonic, sleep=time.sleep,\r\n                 budget=None, deadline=None, default_completion_tokens=1024,\r\n                 cache_context=None):\r\n        if not _integer(default_completion_tokens) or default_completion_tokens < 1:\r\n            raise LLMError(\"configuration\", \"Default completion allowance must be positive.\")\r\n        self.transport = transport if transport is not None else ProcessTransport()\r\n        self.clock, self.sleep = clock, sleep\r\n        self.deadline = deadline or Deadline(clock)\r\n        self.budget = budget if budget is not None else Budget()\r\n        self.default_completion_tokens = default_completion_tokens\r\n        self.cache_context = cache_context or {}\r\n        self.cache = {}\r\n        self.cache_hits = 0\r\n        self.last_metadata = None\r\n        self.lock = threading.RLock()  # Serial dispatch; Budget lock also protects shared clients.\r\n        self._credential = None\r\n        self._fatal = False\r\n\r\n    def _configuration(self, model):\r\n        base, key, configured_model = (os.environ.get(n, \"\") for n in\r\n                                       (\"LLM_BASE_URL\", \"LLM_API_KEY\", \"LLM_MODEL\"))\r\n        if not all(v.strip() for v in (base, key, configured_model)):\r\n            raise LLMError(\"configuration\", \"Set LLM_BASE_URL, LLM_API_KEY and LLM_MODEL.\")\r\n        try:\r\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\r\n\"\"\"Production pipeline: python3 solution.py <input_dir> <output.json>.\r\nUse run.sh on Linux for the outer process-group deadline supervisor.\r\n\"\"\"\r\nimport json\r\nimport os\r\nimport sys\r\nimport uuid\r\nimport time\r\nfrom pathlib import Path\r\n\r\nfrom data import load_input, build_windows\r\nfrom llm import Client, Budget, Deadline, LLMError\r\nfrom validation import SchemaValidator, read_json, validate_output\r\n\r\n\r\ndef assemble_output(data, client=None, deadline=None, summary=None):\r\n    \"\"\"Run existing stages, gate prerequisites, and retain diagnostics separately.\"\"\"\r\n    from reasoning import (extract_windows, resolve_organisations, resolve_entities,\r\n                           resolve_goals, review_task_packet, resolve_help)\r\n    from tasks import build_ledger\r\n    data.require_complete()\r\n    deadline = deadline or Deadline()\r\n    summary = summary if summary is not None else {}\r\n    summary.update(state=\"running\", stages={})\r\n    output = {key: [] for key in (\"people\", \"organisations\", \"chats\", \"goals\", \"open_loops\")}\r\n    if not data.chats_by_id and not data.messages_by_id and not any(data.seed.values()):\r\n        summary[\"state\"] = \"succeeded_empty\"\r\n        return output\r\n    if not data.messages_by_id and any(data.seed.get(k) for k in (\"goals\", \"organisations\")):\r\n        raise ValueError(\"Incomplete: seed-only intentions/entities require message evidence and review.\")\r\n    if client is None:\r\n        try:\r\n            ceiling = os.environ.get(\"LLM_TOKEN_CEILING\")\r\n            planned = os.environ.get(\"LLM_PLANNED_BASE_CALLS\")\r\n            budget = Budget(None if ceiling is None else int(ceiling),\r\n                            None if planned is None else int(planned))\r\n        except ValueError:\r\n            raise ValueError(\"Invalid run budget configuration\") from None\r\n        client = Client(budget=budget, deadline=deadline)\r\n        client._configuration(None)  # Fail before stage work on missing credentials.\r\n    else:\r\n        client.deadline = deadline\r\n    def accept(name, result):\r\n        summary[\"stages\"][name] = result\r\n        summary[\"usage\"] = client.budget.summary()\r\n        if result[\"state\"] not in (\"succeeded\", \"succeeded_empty\"):\r\n            summary[\"state\"] = \"incomplete\"\r\n            raise ValueError(\"Incomplete stage: \" + name)\r\n        deadline.check(finalizing=True)\r\n        return result\r\n    plan = build_windows(data)\r\n    plan.require_complete()\r\n    extraction = accept(\"extraction\", extract_windows(data, plan, client, timezone_policy=\"UTC\"))\r\n    organisations = accept(\"organisations\", resolve_organisations(data, extraction, client))\r\n    entities = accept(\"entities\", resolve_entities(data, extraction, organisations, client))\r\n    goals = accept(\"goals\", resolve_goals(data, extraction, client))\r\n    ledger = accept(\"tasks\", build_ledger(data, extraction,\r\n                    lambda packet, validate: review_task_packet(client, packet, validate)))\r\n    output.update(people=entities[\"people\"], organisations=organisations[\"items\"],\r\n                  chats=entities[\"chats\"], goals=goals[\"items\"])\r\n    for task in ledger[\"tasks\"]:\r\n        item = {\"title\": task[\"action\"] + \" \" + task[\"object\"], \"type\": task[\"type\"],\r\n                \"owner\": task[\"owner\"], \"chat_id\": task[\"chat_id\"],\r\n                \"source_msg\": task[\"source_msg\"], \"status\": task[\"status\"], \"due\": task[\"due\"]}\r\n        if task[\"closed_by\"] is not None:\r\n            item[\"closed_by\"] = task[\"closed_by\"]\r\n        output[\"open_loops\"].append(item)\r\n    output = accept(\"help\", resolve_help(data, output, ledger, client))[\"output\"]\r\n    deadline.check(finalizing=True)\r\n    validate_output(output, data)\r\n    summary[\"state\"] = \"succeeded\"\r\n    summary[\"usage\"] = client.budget.summary()\r\n    return output\r\n\r\n\r\ndef export_output(output, data, output_path, deadline=None):\r\n    \"\"\"Validate, write beside destination, close/revalidate, atomically replace.\r\n\r\n    Exclusive creation avoids clobbering another writer's temporary file. No\r\n    final path is opened for writing. Ordinary failures and KeyboardInterrupt\r\n    clean up owned temporaries; an uncatchable kill can leave an unpublished temp.\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n    if deadline is not None:\r\n        deadline.check(finalizing=True)\r\n    validator = SchemaValidator()\r\n    validate_output(output, data, validator)\r\n    destination = Path(output_path).absolute()\r\n    temporary = destination.with_name(\".\" + destination.name + \".\" + uuid.uuid4().hex + \".tmp\")\r\n    owned = False\r\n    try:\r\n        with temporary.open(\"x\", encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\\n\") as stream:\r\n            owned = True\r\n            json.dump(output, stream, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False)\r\n            stream.write(\"\\n\")\r\n            stream.flush()\r\n            os.fsync(stream.fileno())\r\n        with temporary.open(encoding=\"utf-8\") as stream:\r\n            serialized = read_json(stream)\r\n        validate_output(serialized, data, validator)\r\n        if serialized != output:\r\n            raise ValueError(\"Serialized output differs from the validated in-memory output\")\r\n        if deadline is not None:\r\n            deadline.check(finalizing=True)\r\n        os.replace(temporary, destination)\r\n    finally:\r\n        if owned:\r\n            temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef main(input_dir, output_path, *, client=None, deadline=None, summary=None):\r\n    deadline = deadline or Deadline(started=time.monotonic())\r\n    summary = summary if summary is not None else {}\r\n    try:\r\n        deadline.check(finalizing=True)\r\n        data = load_input(input_dir)\r\n        data.require_complete()\r\n        output = assemble_output(data, client=client, deadline=deadline, summary=summary)\r\n        export_output(output, data, output_path, deadline=deadline)\r\n    except (OSError, ValueError, UnicodeError, RecursionError, LLMError) as error:\r\n        summary[\"state\"] = \"incomplete\"\r\n        print(\"Run failed: \" + str(error), file=sys.stderr)\r\n        return 1\r\n    return 0\r\n\r\n\r\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\r\n    if len(sys.argv) != 3:\r\n        print(__doc__, file=sys.stderr)\r\n        sys.exit(2)\r\n    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]))\r\ndef hang_worker(buffer, endpoint, body, key, timeout, max_bytes):\r\n \"reaped\"])\r\n        self.assertEqual(transport.events[-1][\"exitcode\"], 0)\r\n\r\n    def test_real_hung_and_partial_transfer_workers_terminated(self):\r\n        before = {p.pid for p in harness real monotonic 6s cutoff receives Retry-After 7s, requires one dispatch, no sleep/retry and incomplete output | BLOCKED |\n| Worker crash or invalid result | Existing spawned crash/empty tests; harness crash exit 7 and corrupt IPC result, explicit failure and reaped PID | BLOCKED |\n| TERM ignored, force-kill required | Existing synthetic-process escalation test is insufficient; new real worker installs SIG_IGN, requires kill event and exit -SIGKILL | BLOCKED |\n| Hung main, outer launcher | Test-only copy of run.sh changes only 1770/2s to 12/1s; injected hung main and non-detached TERM-ignoring child; requires exit 137 and no surviving/zombie group members | BLOCKED |\n| Normal successful launcher | Unmodified production run.sh, pinned existing Python 3.9.25, synthetic truly empty input; requires exit 0 and valid five-array output | BLOCKED |\n| Interruption during temp write | Actual production export with test-only slow json.dump after partial flush; shortened launcher TERM; requires exit 124, injection marker and unchanged final hash | BLOCKED |\n\nThe harness records distribution, interpreter, timeout version, spawn method,\ncommands, real monotonic elapsed times, dispatch/cutoff/terminate/kill/reap\nevents, worker exit codes, marker PIDs/PGIDs, response progress, stage outcome,\nand before/after hashes. It polls `/proc` for surviving group members including\nzombies. It never treats emergency cleanup as a successful acceptance result.\nAn uncatchable interruption may leave an unpublished temporary, consistent with\nthe existing export contract; the final output must remain unchanged.\n\nAcceptance bounds are fixed in advance: worker attempt 3s, worker grace 0.2s\nper terminate/kill join, scheduling tolerance 2s; application network/exit\nsettings 6/10s; outer TERM 12s, KILL grace 1s, outer cleanup bound strictly\nbelow 15s; independent subprocess emergency timeout 20s. Normal launcher\nsuccess must finish before the harness emergency limit. No bound is widened\nautomatically on failure. Failure records are not proof of process cleanup;\ninspect them and remaining processes before accepting any rerun.\n\nProduction constants are checked separately: network 1620s, application and\nlauncher target 1770s, launcher kill grace 2s, overall allowance 1800s. This\nleaves 150s from network cutoff to finalization target and 28s after nominal\nforce-kill for startup/cleanup scheduling; those margins are budgets, not\nmeasured worst-case guarantees. Accelerated tests do not establish live latency.\n\n## Offline packaging\n\nEstablished Ubuntu full-suite/volume evidence supports the existing stdlib-only\nsix-module deployment and single scoped schema validator.
